# Paper re-analysis (reviewer round 1)

Generated by `scripts/evaluation/paper_reanalysis.py`; every number is in `outputs/paper_reanalysis_manifest.json`. Differences are first model minus second, over the 44 canonical routes unless stated. Percentages are percentage points. W = Wilcoxon statistic, r_rb = matched-pairs rank-biserial, "1st>/</=" = routes where the first model is higher/lower/equal.

**Holm, 2-test family** (H1 two-sided; variant with one-sided H1 in manifest)

| test | raw p | Holm p |
|---|---|---|
| H1: Golden-1600 vs Base DS, two-sided Wilcoxon | 0.0076 | 0.0076 |
| H2: Golden-6400 vs Flooded-6400-Matched standstill | 7.09e-04 | 0.0014 |

H1's p-value was known before the family was declared, so the correction adds no protection for H2 (ANALYSIS_ADDENDUM.md section 1).

## E. What binds at standstill with the gate inactive

Standstill steps (speed < 0.1 m/s) with the gate inactive, classified by the offline pre-gate target speed and the logged (smoothed, mutually exclusive) brake/throttle.

Approximations: Chord lengths (lookahead index, v_target_planned, v_downstream, v_extent_limit) use the logged local x-y waypoints; the agent uses world x-y after a rigid transform. Identical when ego pitch and roll are zero; otherwise the world x-y chord is shorter by the projection of the tilt. Turn angle uses the local frame as in the agent (abs(y), so the y-flip is irrelevant). speed is the logged speedometer value, the same variable the agent uses (current_speed). The PID output itself is not re-simulated; brake/throttle are the logged final commands.

## G. Vulnerable-road-user routes

Route XML <waypoints> keypoints joined by straight segments in x-y; each <trigger_point> is projected onto the nearest segment and its arc length divided by the polyline length gives the fraction. "Reached" means score_route >= that fraction. Approximate: the leaderboard measures completion on its own road-network interpolation of the keypoints (meta.route_length), so the fraction is only as exact as the polyline-to-route_length ratio and the keypoint spacing reported per route.

## I. Threshold sweep

Ported from stress_test_paper.py (tests 1-2): plain L < t and speed < v, per-route % of logged steps, two-sided Wilcoxon; comparator set extended to matched and historical.

24 specifications; min p = 1.69e-10, max p = 0.0160.

## J. Proceed-cued standstill (keyword heuristic)

KEYWORD HEURISTIC, not a validated classifier. Lower-cased coc text; CAUTION if any caution pattern matches; PROCEED if any proceed pattern matches and no caution pattern; else OTHER. Patterns are Python regexes (\b = word boundary, so "stop" does not match "stopped").

Caution patterns: `['\\bstop\\b', '\\byield', '\\bwait', 'keep distance', 'slow down', '\\bred\\b', '\\bobstacle']`

Proceed patterns: `['\\bclear\\b', '\\bproceed', '\\bcontinue', 'keep lane', '\\baccelerat', '\\bnudge', '\\bgo\\b', '\\bresume']`

## M. Harmonised 60 s termination rule

Truncate each step log after the first step that completes 1,200 consecutive logged steps with speed < 0.1 m/s (harness: >1,200 ticks of 3-D velocity < 0.1). Driving Score cannot be harmonised this way (infractions and completion after the cut are unknown).

## U. Driven standstill ("blocked" time), addendum section 8

Status: complete

post hoc, exploratory; written down (section 8) after a per-episode listing of long stops had been seen

driven: speed < 0.1 m/s, throttle >= 0.35, brake == 0 (logged final command); blocked: driven steps in a run of >= 100 consecutive driven steps; driven_transient: driven steps not in such a run; not_driven: all other standstill steps; caveat: inferred from the command log, not from a contact sensor

## W. Motion-history swap test (addendum sections 11 and 11-B)

Status: complete

new runs, exploratory; outcomes and reading rule fixed in addendum section 11-B before any swap run existed

swap steps: about one step in five from step 60; plan logged, not acted on; low speed: speed <= 0.5 m/s, swap = moving history (4.0 m/s straight); moving: speed > 0.5 m/s, swap = stationary history; v_start: |first waypoint| / 0.1 s; v_end: path length between waypoints 54 and 64 / 1.0 s; dv: v_end - v_start (planned speed change; set by the learned controls, not by the initial speed); per_route_rule: a route is used only if it has at least 20 steps in both conditions
